the saved case objects; overlapping classifications are not summed into the
462-case total.

## 8.4 Data and Tool Coverage

- Required data: server-authorized account and scope; the full lifecycle
  candidate stream ordered by raw UTC then stable ID; candidate states and
  open/decision/incomplete coverage; account IANA timezone and local-day
  mapping; compatible gross or fee-complete net realized P/L and currency;
  effective saved rules, versions, scope, applicability, and factual
  determinations; explicit trader-authored mistake, setup, and plan labels;
  Category 6 size facts, Category 7 interval facts, Category 8 repeat ordinals,
  the eventual Category 10 candle giveback result, and an approved versioned
  historical-comparison contract and result for `mistake_cost`.
- Optional data: privacy-safe evidence summaries, sample and barrier counts,
  numerator and denominator detail, taxonomy versions, and bounded result
  metadata that does not expose private notes or identifiers.
- Missing data: Category 10's exact candle `profit_giveback` contract remains a
  later dependency; an approved historical-comparison contract/result may be
  absent; rule, label, applicability, fee, currency, order, time, unit, or
  decision facts may be missing or conflicting. Each affected result must be
  partial or unavailable rather than guessed.
- Tool targets: future deterministic, read-only Journal Analytics queries that
  consume Category-owned facts and return coverage with authorized results.
- Tools not yet implemented: a Category 9 AI Chat router or handler, named
  behaviour-metric queries, Category 10's candle calculation until accepted,
  and any historical-comparison executor until its contract is separately
  approved and implemented.
- Unsupported capabilities: diagnosing motive, discipline, character, or loss
  chasing; proving causation; prediction or advice; defaulting thresholds,
  baselines, rules, versions, or aliases; inventing missing facts; mixing
  accounts, currencies, or incompatible units; exposing private evidence; or
  changing rules, settings, labels, or Journal facts from a read-only request.

## 8.5 Overlap Review

- Duplicate concepts found: none within the controlling Category 9 list.
  `profit_giveback` is a routing/reference record to Category 10's sole exact
  candle calculation, not a second realized-path or behaviour calculation.
- Synonym collisions: `trade` may mean execution, lifecycle, position, or
  order; `loss`, `win`, `large`, `repeat`, `stop`, `target`, `cutoff`,
  `mistake`, `setup`, and `plan` require the recorded grain, basis, threshold,
  rule, or label context supplied by their canonical records.
- Cross-category conflicts: Category 8 owns lifecycle sequence and repeat
  ordinals; Category 7 owns intervals; Category 6 owns quantity/size facts;
  Categories 2 and 3 own realized P/L and outcome facts; Category 5 owns fee
  completeness; Category 10 owns candle giveback; trader-authored rules and
  labels own applicability and followed/broken facts.
- Terms requiring global ambiguity policy: overtrading, fourth trade, after a
  loss or win, large win or loss, loss chasing, larger size, shorter wait,
  profit giveback, limit, target, stop, cutoff, mistake, setup discipline, and
  plan adherence.
- Terms requiring user-defined aliases: none accepted globally. Saved rule,
  setup, plan, and taxonomy identities may be clarified only where genuinely
  ambiguous; a request cannot overwrite effective historical facts.

## 8.6 Remaining Gaps

1. All 21 capabilities remain `Planned`; no Category 9 AI Chat or analytics
   runtime is claimed.
2. `profit_giveback` remains unavailable until Category 10's exact candle
   contract and result are accepted; Category 9 must not duplicate it.
3. `mistake_cost` remains unavailable without a fully specified, approved,
   versioned historical-comparison contract and its result. Factual P/L on
   labelled mistakes is separate and is not proof that a mistake caused a
   difference.
4. Rule metrics remain partial or unavailable where the historically effective
   rule version, scope, event grain, comparator, threshold, basis, currency, or
   applicability facts are missing or conflicting. A read-only request cannot
   borrow a current/later rule or rewrite stored semantics.
5. Mistake, setup, and plan metrics remain partial or unavailable without
   explicit trader-authored versioned labels, applicability, provenance, and
   factual determinations required by the relevant definition.
6. Lifecycle, P/L, fee, currency, timezone, unit, sequence, open-position,
   decision, or incomplete-record gaps remain visible as metric-specific
   partial or unavailable coverage; barriers are never skipped.

These are the complete known non-blocking implementation and data gaps at the
Version 1 `Complete` documentation boundary.

# 10. Review Notes

## Reviewer Findings

Independent review returned PASS for the exact 21-item planning inventory and
its remediation. The controller accepted that planning boundary and authorized
canonical Batches 1 through 3 for `C9-BEH-001` through `C9-BEH-021`.
Independent review returned PASS for all 21 canonical records. Registry
Batches 1 through 4 for `C9-BEH-001` through `C9-BEH-021` received independent
review with required Section 6 remediation. The requested intent precedence,
historical-comparison interpretation, effective-rule-version selection, and
one-field clarification corrections are applied. The final two requested
corrections also preserve C9-BEH-013's unconditional inclusive comparator and
make C9-BEH-016 grain/comparator facts read-only from the effective saved rule.
The remediated registries independently passed. Evaluation Batches 1 through 7
for `C9-E1` through `C9-E21` and the Section 8 coverage report passed
comprehensive independent Terra review. All 462 cases were reviewed and passed
with 0 failures: 21 clarification, 21 unsupported, 21 cross-category, 0
confirmation, and 0 protected-action cases. The exact schema correction from
`ambiguous` to required case type `ambiguity` at position 18 in all 21 arrays
validated without changing inputs or semantics. The controller accepted the
canonical, registry, evaluation, and coverage gates. Clerical Terra review also
passed. The controller then approved and locked all 21 canonical names and
registries, assigned Version 1 to the metadata and every record, accepted the
synchronized master-tracker transition, and marked Category 9 Complete without
changing any metric semantic, evaluation array, `Planned` status, or runtime
boundary.

## Required Changes

None. Comprehensive and clerical Terra review passed, and the controller
accepted the final approval, lock, Version 1, master-tracker, and Complete
gates. No runtime implementation is authorized by documentation completion.

## Completed Changes

Completed and controller-accepted the exact planning inventory after
independent PASS. Added all required canonical fields and Related Concepts for
